24	  const colWidth = Math.floor((totalWidth - 22) / 2)

26	  const handleChoose = async (file: FirstPageIn) => {

30	
31	  const handleMerge = async () => {
32	    if (!fileA.path || !fileB.path) return
33	    const outPath = await SavePDF()
34	    if (!outPath) return

36	    try {

9	const MIN_TOTAL_WIDTH = 240
10	const MAX_TOTAL_WIDTH = 960
11	export const DEFAULT_TOTAL_WIDTH = DEFAULT_WIDTH * 2

13	export type FirstPageIn = 'a' | 'b'
14	export type SelectedPage = { file: FirstPageIn, page: number }

28	function makePageNumberLabel(isFirst: boolean, countOther: number) {
29	  return (index: number) => {
30	    const n = index + 1
31	    if (n <= countOther) return isFirst ? 2 * n - 1 : 2 * n
32	    return 2 * countOther + (n - countOther)
33	  }
34	}
35	
36	export default function MergeModeThumbnailPanel({
37	  fileA, fileB,
38	  selectedPage, onSelectPage,
39	  firstPageIn, totalWidth, onWidthChange, colWidth, reverseB,
40	}: Props) {
41	  const selectedPageA = selectedPage.file === 'a' ? selectedPage.page : null
42	  const selectedPageB = selectedPage.file === 'b' ? selectedPage.page : null
43	  const thumbWidth = colWidth - ITEM_PADDING * 2
44	  const thumbHeight = Math.round(thumbWidth * PAGE_ASPECT)
45	  const itemHeight = thumbHeight + LABEL_HEIGHT + ITEM_PADDING
46	
47	  const bothLoaded = fileA.path !== null && fileB.path !== null
48	  const halfThumbHeight = Math.round(thumbHeight / 2)
49	  const offsetA = bothLoaded && firstPageIn === 'b' ? halfThumbHeight : 0
50	  const offsetB = bothLoaded && firstPageIn === 'a' ? halfThumbHeight : 0
51	
52	  const totalHeight = Math.max(offsetA + fileA.count * itemHeight, offsetB + fileB.count * itemHeight, 0)
53	
54	  const scrollRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
55	
56	  const loaderA = usePageLoader(fileA.path ?? '', thumbWidth)
57	  const loaderB = usePageLoader(fileB.path ?? '', thumbWidth)
58	
59	  const aIsFirst = firstPageIn === 'a'
60	  const pageLabelA = bothLoaded ? makePageNumberLabel(aIsFirst, aIsFirst ? fileB.count : fileA.count) : undefined
61	  const pageLabelB = bothLoaded ? makePageNumberLabel(!aIsFirst, aIsFirst ? fileA.count : fileB.count) : undefined
62	
63	  const startDrag = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
64	    const startX = e.clientX
65	    const startWidth = totalWidth
66	    const clamp = (w: number) => Math.max(MIN_TOTAL_WIDTH, Math.min(MAX_TOTAL_WIDTH, w))
67	
68	    const onMove = (ev: MouseEvent) => onWidthChange(clamp(startWidth + ev.clientX - startX))
69	    const onUp = (ev: MouseEvent) => {
70	      onWidthChange(clamp(startWidth + ev.clientX - startX))
71	      loaderA.invalidate()
72	      loaderB.invalidate()
73	      document.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMove)
74	      document.removeEventListener('mouseup', onUp)
75	    }
76	    document.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove)
77	    document.addEventListener('mouseup', onUp)
78	    e.preventDefault()
79	  }
80	
81	  return (
82	    <Box style={{ display: 'flex', height: '100%', flexShrink: 0 }}>
83	      <Box style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', width: totalWidth, height: '100%' }}>
84	        {/* Single scroll area with two absolute columns */}
85	        <div

87	          style={{

90	            overflowY: 'auto',
91	            overflowX: 'hidden',
92	            background: 'var(--mantine-color-gray-3)',
93	          }}
94	        >
95	          <div style={{ height: totalHeight, position: 'relative' }}>
96	            <div style={{ position: 'absolute', left: 0, top: 0, width: colWidth, height: '100%' }}>
97	              <ThumbColumn

99	                file={fileA}
100	                itemHeight={itemHeight}
101	                paddingStart={offsetA}
102	                thumbHeight={thumbHeight}
103	                loader={loaderA}
104	                selectedPage={selectedPageA}
105	                onSelectPage={(page) => onSelectPage('a', page)}
106	                pageLabel={pageLabelA}
107	              />
108	            </div>
109	            <div style={{ position: 'absolute', left: colWidth, top: 0, width: colWidth, height: '100%' }}>
110	              <ThumbColumn
111	                scrollRef={scrollRef}
112	                file={fileB}
113	                itemHeight={itemHeight}
114	                paddingStart={offsetB}
115	                thumbHeight={thumbHeight}
116	                loader={loaderB}
117	                selectedPage={selectedPageB}
118	                onSelectPage={(page) => onSelectPage('b', page)}
119	                pageLabel={pageLabelB}
120	                reverse={reverseB}
121	              />
122	            </div>
123	          </div>
124	        </div>
125	      </Box>
126	
127	      {/* Resize drag handle */}
128	      <div
129	        onMouseDown={startDrag}
130	        style={{
131	          width: DRAG_HANDLE_WIDTH,
132	          height: '100%',
133	          cursor: 'col-resize',
134	          flexShrink: 0,
135	          background: 'var(--mantine-color-gray-3)',
136	        }}
137	      />
138	    </Box>
139	  )
140	}
141	
142	interface ThumbColumnProps {
143	  scrollRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>
144	  file: PDFFile
145	  itemHeight: number
146	  paddingStart: number
147	  thumbHeight: number
148	  loader: PageLoader
149	  selectedPage: number | null
150	  onSelectPage: (page: number) => void
151	  pageLabel?: (index: number) => number
152	  reverse?: boolean
153	}
154	
155	function ThumbColumn({
156	  scrollRef, file, itemHeight, paddingStart,
157	  thumbHeight, loader,
158	  selectedPage, onSelectPage, pageLabel,
159	  reverse,
160	}: ThumbColumnProps) {
161	  const { count, skipped, rotations, toggleSkip, rotate } = file
162	  const pageAt = (index: number) => reverse ? count - index : index + 1
163	  const [hoveredPage, setHoveredPage] = useState<number | null>(null)
164	  const virtualizer = useVirtualizer({
165	    count,
166	    getScrollElement: () => scrollRef.current,
167	    estimateSize: () => itemHeight,
168	    overscan: 3,
169	    paddingStart,
170	  })
171	
172	  useEffect(() => {
173	    virtualizer.measure()
174	    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
175	  }, [itemHeight])
176	
177	  useEffect(() => {
178	    for (const item of virtualizer.getVirtualItems()) loader.load(pageAt(item.index))
179	  })
180	
181	  useEffect(() => {
182	    if (selectedPage !== null && count > 0) {
183	      const displayIndex = reverse ? count - selectedPage : selectedPage - 1
184	      virtualizer.scrollToIndex(displayIndex, { align: 'auto' })
185	    }
186	    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
187	  }, [selectedPage])
188	
189	  return virtualizer.getVirtualItems().map(item => {
190	    const page = pageAt(item.index)
191	    const src = loader.getSrc(page)
192	    const isSelected = page === selectedPage
193	    const isSkipped = skipped.has(page)
194	    const showSkipBtn = hoveredPage === page || isSkipped
195	    const rotation = rotations.get(page) ?? 0
196	    const isRotated = rotation !== 0
197	    const showRotateBtn = hoveredPage === page || isRotated
198	    const isOddRotation = rotation === 90 || rotation === 270
199	    const imgTransform = rotation ? `rotate(${rotation}deg)${isOddRotation ? ` scale(${210 / 297})` : ''}` : undefined
200	    return (
201	      <div
202	        key={item.key}
203	        onClick={() => onSelectPage(page)}
204	        onMouseEnter={() => setHoveredPage(page)}
205	        onMouseLeave={() => setHoveredPage(null)}
206	        style={{
207	          position: 'absolute',
208	          top: item.start,
209	          left: 0,
210	          width: '100%',
211	          height: item.size,
212	          padding: ITEM_PADDING,
213	          paddingBottom: 0,
214	          boxSizing: 'border-box',
215	          cursor: 'pointer',
216	        }}
217	      >
218	        <div style={{
219	          position: 'relative',
220	          border: `2px solid ${isSelected ? 'var(--mantine-color-blue-5)' : 'transparent'}`,
221	          borderRadius: 4,
222	        }}>
223	          <div style={{ overflow: 'hidden', borderRadius: 2, background: 'var(--mantine-color-gray-1)' }}>
224	            {src ? (
225	              <img src={src} alt={`page ${page}`} style={{ width: '100%', display: 'block', opacity: isSkipped ? 0.3 : 1, transform: imgTransform }} draggable={false} />
226	            ) : (
227	              <div style={{ width: '100%', height: thumbHeight, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
228	                {loader.isLoading(page) && <Loader size="xs" />}
229	              </div>
230	            )}
231	          </div>
232	          {showRotateBtn && (
233	            <div
234	              onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); rotate(page) }}
235	              style={{
236	                position: 'absolute', top: 3, left: 3,
237	                width: 16, height: 16, borderRadius: 3,
238	                background: isRotated ? 'var(--mantine-color-blue-6)' : 'rgba(0,0,0,0.45)',
239	                display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
240	                cursor: 'pointer', color: 'white',
241	              }}
242	            >
243	              <IconRotateClockwise size={10} stroke={3} />
244	            </div>
245	          )}
246	          {showSkipBtn && (
247	            <div
248	              onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); toggleSkip(page) }}
249	              style={{
250	                position: 'absolute', top: 3, right: 3,
251	                width: 16, height: 16, borderRadius: 3,
252	                background: isSkipped ? 'var(--mantine-color-orange-6)' : 'rgba(0,0,0,0.45)',
253	                display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
254	                cursor: 'pointer', color: 'white',
255	              }}
256	            >
257	              <IconX size={10} stroke={3} />
258	            </div>
259	          )}
260	        </div>
261	        <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', fontSize: 11, color: isSkipped ? 'var(--mantine-color-gray-5)' : 'var(--mantine-color-gray-7)', height: LABEL_HEIGHT, lineHeight: `${LABEL_HEIGHT}px` }}>
262	          {pageLabel ? pageLabel(item.index) : page}
263	        </div>
264	      </div>
265	    )
266	  })
267	}

> AGENT

Here's my honest assessment of the complexity:

**What reordering would require:**

1. **State**: Replace the implicit `1..count` page order with an explicit `pageOrder: number[]` per file. The `pageAt(index)` mapping in `ThumbColumn` already abstracts this — you'd change it from `reverse ? count - index : index + 1` to `pageOrder[index]`. The `reverseB` checkbox becomes a convenience initializer for that array.

2. **Backend**: `MergePDFs` currently takes `reverseB bool` and derives the sequence. You'd pass explicit `[]int` page sequences for A and B instead — a straightforward change.

3. **UI interaction**: This is where the complexity lives. Two realistic options:
   - **Arrow buttons** (up/down on hover, like skip/rotate) — ~30 lines of code, zero new dependencies, but painful for more than a few reorders
   - **Drag-and-drop** — feels right, but `@dnd-kit/sortable` + the virtualizer's absolute-positioned layout is a real integration challenge. The virtualizer renders items at absolute `top` positions, not in flow — DnD libraries expect flow layout for measuring drop targets.

**My take:**

Arrow buttons are probably the right call here. The use case for this tool is fixing scan order mistakes, not arbitrary creative reordering — you're likely moving a page one or two slots, not shuffling 50 pages. Arrow buttons handle that cleanly with no new dependencies, and the state change is small (swap two elements in the `pageOrder` array). 

Drag-and-drop would add real implementation risk (virtualizer ↔ DnD integration is notoriously fiddly) for a marginal UX gain on what is probably a rare operation.

> DEVELOPER

Let's skip reordering for now, I don't see the usecase. Update spec.md, ticking of step 6 and moving reordering to "out of scope / maybe"

```
1	# Paper Scan Processor
2	
3	A desktop application for post-processing PDF files containing batches of scanned documents. The app has two modes: **Merge** and **Split**.
4	
5	## Tech stack
6	
7	- **Desktop framework**: Wails
8	- **Backend**: Go
9	- **Frontend**: TypeScript + React
10	- **UI components**: Mantine + `@mantine/dropzone`
11	- **Virtualization**: TanStack Virtual
12	- **Drag-and-drop**: dnd-kit
13	- **State**: Zustand
14	- **PDF manipulation**: pdfcpu
15	- **PDF rendering** (thumbnails): `mutool` (system install, called as subprocess)
16	
17	## Common elements
18	
19	Both modes include a thumbnail panel and a page detail panel, and support the same per-page editing operations.
20	
21	### Thumbnail panel
22	
23	A vertically scrolling strip of page thumbnails. Width is user-adjustable via a drag handle; thumbnails scale to fill the panel width. Rendering is on-demand and virtualized — only visible thumbnails (plus a small overscan buffer) are rendered at any given time.
24	
25	The Go backend exposes a per-page render method returning a base64-encoded PNG; the frontend requests thumbnails as they scroll into view (`mutool draw` subprocess).
26	
27	#### Keyboard shortcuts
28	
29	| Key                    | Action                                     |
30	| ---------------------- | ------------------------------------------ |
31	| `R`                    | Rotate selected page clockwise 90°         |
32	| `Shift+R`              | Rotate selected page counter-clockwise 90° |
33	| `Delete` / `Backspace` | Toggle skip on the selected page           |
34	| `←` / `→`              | Select previous / next page                |
35	
36	### Page detail panel
37	
38	Shows the currently selected page at reading resolution. Selecting a thumbnail updates it.
39	
40	Supports:
41	
42	- **Pan**: drag to pan.
43	- **Zoom**: scroll wheel or trackpad pinch to zoom in/out.
44	- **Navigate**: `←` / `→` to move to the previous/next page.
45	
46	Implemented with `react-zoom-pan-pinch`.
47	
48	### Page editing
49	
50	Individual pages can be edited in both modes before export or merge:
51	
52	- **Rotation**: pages can be rotated in 90° increments (clockwise or counter-clockwise).
53	- **Skip**: pages can be marked as skipped — excluded from the output but remaining visible in the thumbnail view (greyed out). A page is skipped by clicking a skip icon that appears on hover, or via keyboard shortcut.
54	- **Reorder**: pages can be reordered by dragging thumbnails to a new position.
55	
56	## Mode: Merge
57	
58	For scanners that can only scan one side at a time. The user scans all front pages as one PDF and all back pages as another, then uses Merge mode to interleave them into a single PDF.
59	
60	### Workflow
61	
62	1. The user loads two PDF files, labelled **File A** and **File B**.
63	2. The user selects which file contains the first page (**First page in: File A / File B**).
64	3. A **Reverse File B** checkbox controls whether File B is reversed before interleaving. This should be checked when the paper stack was flipped between scans (the typical case, when scanning one side at a time), causing the second-scanned pages to be in reverse order.
65	4. The app interleaves the pages: A1, B1, A2, B2, etc.
66	5. The user saves the merged result as a new PDF file on disk.
67	
68	The merged PDF can then be opened in Split mode for further processing.
69	
70	### Unequal page counts
71	
72	If File A and File B have different page counts, the app shows a warning before proceeding: "File A has X pages, File B has Y pages. The extra Z page(s) will be appended at the end." The user can cancel or continue. The extra pages from the longer file are appended in order after the interleaved section.
73	
74	### Layout
75	
76	Merge mode uses a two-column layout:
77	
78	- **Left panel** — two side-by-side thumbnail strips, one per input file.
79	- **Right panel** (fills remaining space) — the page detail view, showing whichever page was most recently selected in either thumbnail strip.
80	
81	The thumbnail strip for the file containing the second output page is offset downward by half a thumbnail height. This makes the interleaving order visually apparent: the two files' pages appear to slot between each other.
82	
83	```
84	  A               B
85	  ┌──────────┐
86	  │   A1     │
87	  └──────────┘  ┌──────────┐
88	                │   B1     │
89	  ┌──────────┐  └──────────┘
90	  │   A2     │
91	  └──────────┘  ┌──────────┐
92	                │   B2     │
93	  ┌──────────┐  └──────────┘
94	  │   A3     │
95	  └──────────┘
96	```
97	
98	The offset makes the interleaving order visually apparent without needing labels.
99	
100	### Error handling
101	
102	TBD.
103	
104	## Mode: Split
105	
106	### Workflow
107	
108	1. The user loads an input PDF via a file picker dialog or by dragging and dropping a file onto the app window.
109	2. The app displays all pages as thumbnails in the left panel. Clicking a thumbnail selects it and updates the detail panel on the right.
110	3. The user defines split points by clicking in the gaps between page thumbnails. A visual divider appears at each split point; clicking again removes it. Dividers can also be repositioned by dragging them to a different gap. Each divider marks where a new output file begins.
111	4. The user sets a global filename template using `{date}` (today's date as `YYYY-MM-DD`) and `{name}` (a per-file label). Example: `{date} {name}` → `2026-06-12 invoice.pdf`. The app prefills the filename for each output file using this template. The user can then edit any individual file's name before exporting.
112	5. The user sets a global output folder (an existing folder on the local filesystem, or a Google Drive folder if Drive integration is enabled). For each output file, the user can adjust:
113	   - The prefilled filename (editable)
114	   - The destination folder (overridable per file)
115	6. The user clicks Export. Before splitting, the app checks for filename conflicts at each destination. If any conflict is found, the export is aborted and an error message identifies the conflicting files. Once resolved, the app splits the input PDF and writes (or uploads) each output file. Afterwards, the app prompts the user to keep, move, or delete the input file.
116	
117	### Layout
118	
119	Split mode uses a two-column layout:
120	
121	- **Left panel** (adjustable width, drag handle on right edge) — a single vertically scrolling area that combines the thumbnail strip and output file controls.
122	- **Right panel** (fills remaining space) — the page detail view.
123	
124	#### Left panel structure
125	
126	The left panel is a continuous scroll area. Pages are grouped by output file. Each group is preceded by a compact **output file header** containing the editable filename field and (when applicable) destination folder. Split-point dividers between groups are the visual boundary between one output file and the next.
127	
128	```
129	┌─ invoice.pdf ──────────────────────┐
130	│ folder: /Documents                 │
131	└────────────────────────────────────┘
132	  [page 1 thumbnail]
133	  [page 2 thumbnail]
134	  [page 3 thumbnail]
135	  ──────── [gap / split point] ───────
136	┌─ receipt.pdf ──────────────────────┐
137	│ folder: /Documents                 │
138	└────────────────────────────────────┘
139	  [page 4 thumbnail]
140	  [page 5 thumbnail]
141	```
142	
143	Clicking a gap toggles a split point there and creates a new output file section. The filename and folder fields for each section appear immediately above its pages.
144	
145	### Keyboard shortcuts
146	
147	| Key     | Action                                                  |
148	| ------- | ------------------------------------------------------- |
149	| `Space` | Toggle a split point after the selected page            |
150	| `Tab`   | Move focus to the next filename input in the left panel |
151	
152	### Google Drive integration (optional)
153	
154	- Authentication uses Google OAuth via a browser window, triggered the first time Drive is enabled. Credentials are stored locally and reused in future sessions.
155	- When Drive is enabled, each output file header in the left panel shows a Drive folder field. Clicking it opens a folder browser modal that displays the user's Drive folder tree. The user navigates the tree and selects a destination folder. The selected path is shown in the header (e.g. `My Drive / Clients / Smith / 2026`).
156	- The folder tree is fetched lazily — the first time the user opens the folder browser in a session.
157	- The folder browser shows a **recently used folders** list at the top for quick access. This list persists across sessions.
158	- The global output folder setting can be set to a Drive folder, which prefills all output file headers. Individual headers can be overridden.
159	- After export, each output file is uploaded to its designated Drive folder. If an upload fails, the affected header shows an inline error message and a Retry button; other uploads are unaffected.
160	- When Drive is enabled, the local subfolder name for each output file is derived automatically from the Drive folder path (see Local folder sorting below).
161	
162	### Local folder sorting
163	
164	Each output file can be assigned a subfolder name. The file is then saved to `[output folder] / [subfolder] / filename.pdf` rather than directly into the output folder.
165	
166	When Drive is enabled, the subfolder name is derived automatically from the innermost component of the Drive folder path (e.g. `My Drive / Clients / Smith / 2026` → subfolder `2026`). When Drive is disabled, the user enters the subfolder name manually in the left panel header.
167	
168	If two or more output files in the same batch share the same subfolder name, the app prefixes the parent folder name to disambiguate. If the parent name is also shared, it walks further up the hierarchy until uniqueness is achieved. Examples:
169	
170	- `Clients / Smith / 2026` and `Clients / Jones / 2026` → `Smith - 2026` and `Jones - 2026`
171	- `Clients / Smith / 2026` and `Archive / Smith / 2026` → `Clients - Smith - 2026` and `Archive - Smith - 2026`
172	
173	(When Drive is disabled and names are entered manually, the user is responsible for avoiding conflicts — no automatic disambiguation applies.)
174	
175	To ensure disambiguation is fully deterministic, **the output folder must be empty when the input PDF is opened**. The app enforces this and shows an error if the folder is not empty.
176	
177	### Persisted settings
178	
179	The following settings are saved across sessions:
180	
181	- Last-used output folder
182	- Filename template
183	
184	### Error handling
185	
186	TBD.
187	
188	## Implementation checklist
189	
190	### Primitives
191	
192	- [x] **Go: PDF merge/split** — interleave and split PDFs with pdfcpu; hardcoded paths; `_test.go` harness, no UI
193	- [x] **Frontend: Thumbnail panel** — virtualized vertical scroll, on-demand per-page render via Go/mutool, resizable width with drag handle
194	- [x] **Frontend: Detail panel** — pan and zoom with `react-zoom-pan-pinch`, driven by selected page
195	
196	### Merge mode
197	
198	- [x] **Step 1: End-to-end merge pipeline** — Go `OpenFileDialog` / `SaveFileDialog` RPCs; `MergePDFs` Wails RPC; minimal UI with two file-picker buttons and a Merge & Save button; no thumbnails
199	- [x] **Step 2: Thumbnail strips** — two-column layout using existing `ThumbnailPanel` primitive, one per file, side by side; Merge & Save moves to toolbar
200	- [x] **Step 3: A/B selector + visual offset** — "First page in" toggle (File A / File B); second strip offset down by half a thumbnail height
201	- [x] **Step 4: Reverse checkbox + page-count warning** — "Reverse File B" checkbox wired into merge call; detect unequal counts and show info in toolbar
202	- [x] **Step 5: Detail panel** — existing `DetailPanel` primitive on the right; selection in either strip updates it; `←` / `→` keyboard navigation
203	- [ ] **Step 6: Page editing** — rotate, skip, reorder within the merge view, applied before the merge call
204	
205	### Split mode
206	
207	- [ ] **Go: render + split** — per-page PNG render and PDF split/export backend methods
208	- [ ] **Frontend: Split layout** — resizable left panel + detail panel
209	- [ ] **Frontend: Split points** — click gaps to toggle, drag to reposition, visual dividers
210	- [ ] **Frontend: Output file headers** — filename template, prefill, per-file folder override
211	- [ ] **Frontend: Export flow** — conflict check, keep/move/delete prompt after export
212	- [ ] **Page editing in Split** — rotate, skip, reorder
213	- [ ] **Keyboard shortcuts**
214	
215	### Google Drive integration
216	
217	- [ ] **OAuth authentication** — browser-based sign-in, credentials stored locally and reused across sessions
218	- [ ] **Folder browser modal** — lazy-loaded Drive folder tree, opens when clicking a folder field
219	- [ ] **Recently used folders** — shown at the top of the folder browser, persisted across sessions
220	- [ ] **Upload on export** — per-file upload to designated Drive folder, inline error + Retry on failure
221	- [ ] **Local subfolder derivation** — subfolder name derived automatically from the innermost Drive path component; disambiguation when multiple files share the same leaf name
222	
223	### Polish
224	
225	- [ ] **Persisted settings** — last-used output folder, filename template
226	
227	## Future / out of scope for v1
228	
229	- **Insert pages from another PDF**: allow the user to pull pages from a second PDF into the current document before splitting. Planned for v2.
230	- **Scanning integration**: trigger a scan from within the app using OS or device APIs. Not currently planned but under consideration.

3	
4	export interface PageLoader {
5	  getSrc: (page: number) => string | undefined
6	  isLoading: (page: number) => boolean
7	  isFailed: (page: number) => boolean
8	  load: (page: number) => void
9	  invalidate: () => void
10	}
11	
12	export function usePageLoader(pdfPath: string, widthPx: number): PageLoader {
13	  const cacheRef = useRef(new Map<number, string>())
14	  const loadingRef = useRef(new Set<number>())
15	  const failedRef = useRef(new Set<number>())
16	  const [, setTick] = useState(0)
17	
18	  const invalidate = () => {
19	    cacheRef.current.clear()
20	    loadingRef.current.clear()
21	    failedRef.current.clear()
22	    setTick(t => t + 1)
23	  }
24

26	    invalidate()
27	  // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
28	  }, [pdfPath])
29	
30	  const load = (page: number) => {
31	    if (cacheRef.current.has(page) || loadingRef.current.has(page) || failedRef.current.has(page)) return
32	    loadingRef.current.add(page)
33	    RenderPage(pdfPath, page, widthPx)
34	      .then((b64: string) => {
35	        cacheRef.current.set(page, `data:image/png;base64,${b64}`)
36	        loadingRef.current.delete(page)
37	        setTick(t => t + 1)
38	      })
39	      .catch((err: unknown) => {
40	        console.error(`RenderPage failed for page ${page}:`, err)
41	        loadingRef.current.delete(page)
42	        failedRef.current.add(page)
43	        setTick(t => t + 1)
44	      })
45	  }
46	
47	  return {
48	    getSrc: (page) => cacheRef.current.get(page),
49	    isLoading: (page) => loadingRef.current.has(page),
50	    isFailed: (page) => failedRef.current.has(page),
51	    load,
52	    invalidate,
53	  }
54	}

49:// RenderPage renders a single page of the PDF at path as a PNG and returns it
50:// base64-encoded. widthPx controls the output width; height is scaled proportionally.
51:func (a *App) RenderPage(path string, pageNum int, widthPx int) (string, error) {
52:	tmpDir, err := os.MkdirTemp("", "psp-render-*")
66:		"-png",
67:		"-scale-to-x", strconv.Itoa(widthPx),
76:	data, err := os.ReadFile(outPrefix + ".png")
```

> AGENT

The problem is clear: `RenderPage` is called with the CSS pixel width, but on HiDPI screens `devicePixelRatio` is 2 (or more), so the rendered PNG is half the physical resolution needed and the browser upscales it, causing blur.

The fix is to render at physical pixels and let the browser display at CSS size:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01KEhRL9Q8mwtvtdSDCPYi3g
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ben/git/paper-scan-processor/frontend/src/hooks/usePageLoader.ts",
  "old_string": "    RenderPage(pdfPath, page, widthPx)",
  "new_string": "    RenderPage(pdfPath, page, Math.round(widthPx * window.devicePixelRatio))"
}
```

> TOOL

tool_result
id: toolu_01KEhRL9Q8mwtvtdSDCPYi3g
```
The file /Users/ben/git/paper-scan-processor/frontend/src/hooks/usePageLoader.ts has been updated successfully. (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)
```

> AGENT

The rendered PNG is now twice as wide on a 2× display, but `<img style={{ width: '100%' }}>` constrains it to the CSS column width — so the browser downsamples instead of upsampling, giving crisp rendering. The resize-invalidation path (`loaderA.invalidate()` on drag-handle release) already clears the cache and re-requests at the new width, so that continues to work correctly.

1	import { useRef, useEffect } from 'react'
2	import { TransformWrapper, TransformComponent, ReactZoomPanPinchRef } from 'react-zoom-pan-pinch'
3	import { Center, Loader } from '@mantine/core'
4	import { usePageLoader } from '../hooks/usePageLoader'
5	
6	const DETAIL_WIDTH = 1400
7	
8	interface Props {
9	  pdfPath: string
10	  pageNum: number
11	  pageCount: number
12	  rotation?: number
13	  onNavigate: (page: number) => void
14	  onToggleSkip?: () => void
15	  onRotate?: () => void
16	}
17	
18	export default function DetailPanel({ pdfPath, pageNum, pageCount, rotation = 0, onNavigate, onToggleSkip, onRotate }: Props) {
19	  const { getSrc, isLoading, load } = usePageLoader(pdfPath, DETAIL_WIDTH)
20	  const transformRef = useRef<ReactZoomPanPinchRef>(null)
21	
22	  useEffect(() => {
23	    load(pageNum)
24	  }, [pdfPath, pageNum])
25	
26	  useEffect(() => {
27	    transformRef.current?.resetTransform()
28	  }, [pageNum, rotation])
29	
30	  useEffect(() => {
31	    const handler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
32	      if (e.target instanceof HTMLInputElement || e.target instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement) return
33	      if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
34	        e.preventDefault()
35	        if (pageNum > 1) onNavigate(pageNum - 1)
36	      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') {
37	        e.preventDefault()
38	        if (pageNum < pageCount) onNavigate(pageNum + 1)
39	      } else if (e.key === 'Delete' || e.key === 'Backspace') {
40	        e.preventDefault()
41	        onToggleSkip?.()
42	      } else if (e.key === 'r') {
43	        e.preventDefault()
44	        onRotate?.()
45	      }
46	    }
47	    window.addEventListener('keydown', handler)
48	    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handler)
49	  }, [pageNum, pageCount, onNavigate, onToggleSkip, onRotate])
50	
51	  const src = getSrc(pageNum)
52	
53	  return (
54	    <div
55	      style={{
56	        flex: 1,
57	        height: '100%',
58	        overflow: 'hidden',
59	        position: 'relative',
60	        background: 'var(--mantine-color-gray-1)',
61	        padding: 8,
62	      }}
63	    >
64	      {isLoading(pageNum) && (
65	        <Center style={{ position: 'absolute', inset: 0, zIndex: 1 }}>
66	          <Loader />
67	        </Center>
68	      )}
69	      {src && (
70	        <TransformWrapper ref={transformRef} centerOnInit minScale={0.1} maxScale={8}>
71	          <TransformComponent
72	            wrapperStyle={{ width: '100%', height: '100%' }}
73	            contentStyle={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}
74	          >
75	            <img
76	              src={src}
77	              alt={`Page ${pageNum}`}
78	              draggable={false}
79	              style={{ maxWidth: '100%', maxHeight: '100%', display: 'block', userSelect: 'none', transform: rotation ? `rotate(${rotation}deg)` : undefined }}
80	            />
81	          </TransformComponent>
82	        </TransformWrapper>
83	      )}
84	    </div>
85	  )
86	}
87

1	import { useState } from 'react'
2	import { OpenPDF, PageCount } from '../../wailsjs/go/main/App'
3	
4	export interface PDFFile {
5	  path: string | null
6	  count: number
7	  skipped: Set<number>
8	  rotations: Map<number, number>
9	  load: () => Promise<boolean>
10	  toggleSkip: (page: number) => void
11	  rotate: (page: number) => void
12	}
13	
14	export function usePDFFile(): PDFFile {
15	  const [path, setPath] = useState<string | null>(null)
16	  const [count, setCount] = useState(0)
17	  const [skipped, setSkipped] = useState<Set<number>>(() => new Set())
18	  const [rotations, setRotations] = useState<Map<number, number>>(() => new Map())
19	
20	  const load = async () => {
21	    const p = await OpenPDF()
22	    if (!p) return false
23	    const c = await PageCount(p)
24	    setPath(p)
25	    setCount(c)
26	    setSkipped(new Set())
27	    setRotations(new Map())
28	    return true
29	  }
30	
31	  const toggleSkip = (page: number) => {
32	    setSkipped(prev => {
33	      const next = new Set(prev)
34	      if (next.has(page)) next.delete(page); else next.add(page)
35	      return next
36	    })
37	  }
38	
39	  const rotate = (page: number) => {
40	    setRotations(prev => {
41	      const next = new Map(prev)
42	      const deg = ((next.get(page) ?? 0) + 90) % 360
43	      if (deg === 0) next.delete(page); else next.set(page, deg)
44	      return next
45	    })
46	  }
47	
48	  return { path, count, skipped, rotations, load, toggleSkip, rotate }
49	}
50	
```

> AGENT

The issue is clear. `DetailPanel` owns a single `usePageLoader` instance keyed to `pdfPath`, so switching between `fileA.path` and `fileB.path` fires the `invalidate()` effect and clears the cache.

The fix: lift the loader creation out of `DetailPanel` so MergeMode can maintain two persistent loaders — one per file. I'll make `loader` an optional prop; when provided it's used directly, otherwise DetailPanel creates its own (SplitMode path is unchanged).

1	import { useRef, useState, useEffect, forwardRef, useImperativeHandle } from 'react'

5	import { DEFAULT_WIDTH, DRAG_HANDLE_WIDTH, ITEM_PADDING, PAGE_ASPECT, LABEL_HEIGHT } from '../../constants'
6	
7	const MIN_WIDTH = 120
8	const MAX_WIDTH = 480
9	
10	const STRIP_LABEL_HEIGHT = 28
11	
12	export interface ThumbnailPanelHandle {
13	  scrollTo: (top: number) => void
14	}
15	
16	interface Props {
17	  pdfPath: string
18	  pageCount: number
19	  selectedPage: number   // 1-indexed
20	  onSelectPage: (page: number) => void
21	  label?: string
22	  width?: number
23	  hideDragHandle?: boolean
24	  onWidthChange?: (width: number) => void
25	  onScroll?: (scrollTop: number) => void
26	  hideScrollbar?: boolean
27	  topPadding?: number
28	  bottomPadding?: number
29	  pageNumberLabel?: (index: number) => number
30	}
31	
32	const ThumbnailPanel = forwardRef<ThumbnailPanelHandle, Props>(function ThumbnailPanel(
33	  { pdfPath, pageCount, selectedPage, onSelectPage, label, width: controlledWidth, hideDragHandle, onWidthChange, onScroll, hideScrollbar, topPadding = 0, bottomPadding = 0, pageNumberLabel },
34	  ref,
35	) {
36	  const [internalWidth, setInternalWidth] = useState(DEFAULT_WIDTH)
37	  const panelWidth = controlledWidth ?? internalWidth
38	
39	  const thumbWidth = panelWidth - ITEM_PADDING * 2
40	  const thumbHeight = Math.round(thumbWidth * PAGE_ASPECT)
41	  const itemHeight = thumbHeight + LABEL_HEIGHT + ITEM_PADDING
42	
43	  const scrollRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
44	
45	  const virtualizer = useVirtualizer({
46	    count: pageCount,
47	    getScrollElement: () => scrollRef.current,
48	    estimateSize: () => itemHeight,
49	    overscan: 3,
50	    paddingStart: topPadding,
51	  })
52	
53	  // Re-estimate row heights when panel is resized
54	  useEffect(() => {
55	    virtualizer.measure()
56	    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
57	  }, [itemHeight])
58	
59	  const virtualItems = virtualizer.getVirtualItems()
60	  const { getSrc, isLoading, load, invalidate } = usePageLoader(pdfPath, thumbWidth)
61	
62	  useEffect(() => {
63	    for (const item of virtualItems) load(item.index + 1)
64	  })
65	
66	  // Scroll selected page into view (e.g. after keyboard navigation)
67	  useEffect(() => {
68	    virtualizer.scrollToIndex(selectedPage - 1, { align: 'auto' })
69	    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
70	  }, [selectedPage])
71	
72	  useImperativeHandle(ref, () => ({
73	    scrollTo: (top) => { if (scrollRef.current) scrollRef.current.scrollTop = top },
74	  }))
75	
76	  useEffect(() => {
77	    const handler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
78	      if (e.target instanceof HTMLInputElement || e.target instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement) return
79	      if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
80	        e.preventDefault()
81	        if (selectedPage > 1) onSelectPage(selectedPage - 1)
82	      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') {
83	        e.preventDefault()
84	        if (selectedPage < pageCount) onSelectPage(selectedPage + 1)
85	      }
86	    }
87	    window.addEventListener('keydown', handler)
88	    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handler)
89	  }, [selectedPage, pageCount, onSelectPage])
90	
91	  const startDrag = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
92	    const startX = e.clientX
93	    const startWidth = panelWidth
94	    const clamp = (w: number) => Math.max(MIN_WIDTH, Math.min(MAX_WIDTH, w))
95	
96	    const onMove = (ev: MouseEvent) => {
97	      const w = clamp(startWidth + ev.clientX - startX)
98	      setInternalWidth(w)
99	      onWidthChange?.(w)
100	    }
101	    const onUp = (ev: MouseEvent) => {
102	      const w = clamp(startWidth + ev.clientX - startX)
103	      setInternalWidth(w)
104	      onWidthChange?.(w)
105	      invalidate()
106	      document.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMove)
107	      document.removeEventListener('mouseup', onUp)
108	    }
109	    document.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove)
110	    document.addEventListener('mouseup', onUp)
111	    e.preventDefault()
112	  }
113	
114	  return (
115	    <div style={{ display: 'flex', height: '100%', flexShrink: 0 }}>
116	      <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', width: panelWidth, height: '100%' }}>
117	        {label && (
118	          <div
119	            style={{
120	              height: STRIP_LABEL_HEIGHT,
121	              flexShrink: 0,
122	              display: 'flex',
123	              alignItems: 'center',
124	              justifyContent: 'center',
125	              fontSize: 11,
126	              fontWeight: 600,
127	              color: 'var(--mantine-color-dimmed)',
128	              borderBottom: '1px solid var(--mantine-color-gray-2)',
129	            }}
130	          >
131	            {label}
132	          </div>
133	        )}
134	        <div
135	          ref={scrollRef}
136	          onScroll={(e) => onScroll?.(e.currentTarget.scrollTop)}
137	          className={hideScrollbar ? 'hide-scrollbar' : undefined}
138	          style={{
139	            flex: 1,
140	            minHeight: 0,
141	            overflowY: 'auto',
142	            overflowX: 'hidden',
143	            background: 'var(--mantine-color-gray-3)',
144	          }}
145	        >
146	          <div style={{ height: virtualizer.getTotalSize() + bottomPadding, position: 'relative' }}>
147	            {virtualizer.getVirtualItems().map(item => {
148	              const page = item.index + 1
149	              const src = getSrc(page)
150	              const isSelected = page === selectedPage
151	
152	              return (
153	                <div
154	                  key={item.key}
155	                  style={{
156	                    position: 'absolute',
157	                    top: item.start,
158	                    left: 0,
159	                    width: '100%',
160	                    height: item.size,
161	                    padding: ITEM_PADDING,
162	                    paddingBottom: 0,
163	                    boxSizing: 'border-box',
164	                    cursor: 'pointer',
165	                  }}
166	                  onClick={() => onSelectPage(page)}
167	                >
168	                  <div
169	                    style={{
170	                      border: `2px solid ${isSelected ? 'var(--mantine-color-blue-5)' : 'transparent'}`,
171	                      borderRadius: 4,
172	                      overflow: 'hidden',
173	                      background: 'var(--mantine-color-gray-1)',
174	                    }}
175	                  >
176	                    {src ? (
177	                      <img
178	                        src={src}
179	                        alt={`Page ${page}`}
180	                        style={{ width: '100%', display: 'block' }}
181	                        draggable={false}
182	                      />
183	                    ) : (
184	                      <div
185	                        style={{
186	                          width: '100%',
187	                          height: thumbHeight,
188	                          display: 'flex',
189	                          alignItems: 'center',
190	                          justifyContent: 'center',
191	                        }}
192	                      >
193	                        {isLoading(page) && <Loader size="xs" />}
194	                      </div>
195	                    )}
196	                  </div>
197	                  <div
198	                    style={{
199	                      textAlign: 'center',
200	                      fontSize: 11,
201	                      color: 'var(--mantine-color-gray-7)',
202	                      height: LABEL_HEIGHT,
203	                      lineHeight: `${LABEL_HEIGHT}px`,
204	                    }}
205	                  >
206	                    {pageNumberLabel ? pageNumberLabel(item.index) : page}
207	                  </div>
208	                </div>
209	              )
210	            })}
211	          </div>
212	        </div>
213	      </div>
214	
215	      {/* Resize drag handle */}
216	      {!hideDragHandle && (
217	        <div
218	          onMouseDown={startDrag}
219	          style={{
220	            width: DRAG_HANDLE_WIDTH,
221	            height: '100%',
222	            cursor: 'col-resize',
223	            flexShrink: 0,
224	            background: 'var(--mantine-color-gray-3)',
225	          }}
226	        />
227	      )}
228	    </div>
229	  )
230	})

Good — `isFailed` is defined but never called, so it just needs its signature updated. Now I have the full picture. The plan:

- `usePageLoader(path)` — drop `widthPx`; cache key becomes `${page}:${width}`
- `load(page, width)`, `getSrc(page, width)`, `isLoading(page, width)` — width passed at call site
- `DETAIL_WIDTH` returns to a local constant inside `DetailPanel`; callers drop the import
- Both thumbnail panels pass `thumbWidth` at their existing call sites

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/ben/git/paper-scan-processor/frontend/src/hooks/usePageLoader.ts",
  "content": "import { useRef, useState, useEffect } from 'react'\nimport { RenderPage } from '../../wailsjs/go/main/App'\n\nexport interface PageLoader {\n  getSrc: (page: number, width: number) => string | undefined\n  isLoading: (page: number, width: number) => boolean\n  isFailed: (page: number, width: number) => boolean\n  load: (page: number, width: number) => void\n  invalidate: () => void\n}\n\nexport function usePageLoader(pdfPath: string): PageLoader {\n  const cacheRef = useRef(new Map<string, string>())\n  const loadingRef = useRef(new Set<string>())\n  const failedRef = useRef(new Set<string>())\n  const [, setTick] = useState(0)\n\n  const cacheKey = (page: number, width: number) => `${page}:${width}`\n\n  const invalidate = () => {\n    cacheRef.current.clear()\n    loadingRef.current.clear()\n    failedRef.current.clear()\n    setTick(t => t + 1)\n  }\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    invalidate()\n  // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\n  }, [pdfPath])\n\n  const load = (page: number, width: number) => {\n    const k = cacheKey(page, width)\n    if (cacheRef.current.has(k) || loadingRef.current.has(k) || failedRef.current.has(k)) return\n    loadingRef.current.add(k)\n    RenderPage(pdfPath, page, Math.round(width * window.devicePixelRatio))\n      .then((b64: string) => {\n        cacheRef.current.set(k, `data:image/png;base64,${b64}`)\n        loadingRef.current.delete(k)\n        setTick(t => t + 1)\n      })\n      .catch((err: unknown) => {\n        console.error(`RenderPage failed for page ${page}:`, err)\n        loadingRef.current.delete(k)\n        failedRef.current.add(k)\n        setTick(t => t + 1)\n      })\n  }\n\n  return {\n    getSrc: (page, width) => cacheRef.current.get(cacheKey(page, width)),\n    isLoading: (page, width) => loadingRef.current.has(cacheKey(page, width)),\n    isFailed: (page, width) => failedRef.current.has(cacheKey(page, width)),\n    load,\n    invalidate,\n  }\n}\n"
}
```

142	interface ThumbColumnProps {
143	  scrollRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>
144	  file: PDFFile
145	  itemHeight: number
146	  paddingStart: number
147	  thumbHeight: number
148	  loader: PageLoader
149	  selectedPage: number | null
150	  onSelectPage: (page: number) => void
151	  pageLabel?: (index: number) => number
152	  reverse?: boolean
153	}
154	
155	function ThumbColumn({
156	  scrollRef, file, itemHeight, paddingStart,
157	  thumbHeight, loader,
158	  selectedPage, onSelectPage, pageLabel,
159	  reverse,
160	}: ThumbColumnProps) {
161	  const { count, skipped, rotations, toggleSkip, rotate } = file
162	  const pageAt = (index: number) => reverse ? count - index : index + 1
163	  const [hoveredPage, setHoveredPage] = useState<number | null>(null)
164	  const virtualizer = useVirtualizer({
165	    count,
166	    getScrollElement: () => scrollRef.current,
167	    estimateSize: () => itemHeight,
168	    overscan: 3,
169	    paddingStart,
170	  })
171	
172	  useEffect(() => {
173	    virtualizer.measure()
174	    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
175	  }, [itemHeight])
176	
177	  useEffect(() => {
178	    for (const item of virtualizer.getVirtualItems()) loader.load(pageAt(item.index))
179	  })
180	
181	  useEffect(() => {
182	    if (selectedPage !== null && count > 0) {
183	      const displayIndex = reverse ? count - selectedPage : selectedPage - 1
184	      virtualizer.scrollToIndex(displayIndex, { align: 'auto' })
185	    }
186	    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
187	  }, [selectedPage])
188	
189	  return virtualizer.getVirtualItems().map(item => {
190	    const page = pageAt(item.index)
191	    const src = loader.getSrc(page)
192	    const isSelected = page === selectedPage
193	    const isSkipped = skipped.has(page)
194	    const showSkipBtn = hoveredPage === page || isSkipped
195	    const rotation = rotations.get(page) ?? 0
196	    const isRotated = rotation !== 0
197	    const showRotateBtn = hoveredPage === page || isRotated
198	    const isOddRotation = rotation === 90 || rotation === 270
199	    const imgTransform = rotation ? `rotate(${rotation}deg)${isOddRotation ? ` scale(${210 / 297})` : ''}` : undefined

All call sites are consistent. Everything looks correct — `usePageLoader` takes only `pdfPath`, width flows through `load`/`getSrc`/`isLoading`, and `DETAIL_WIDTH` is back to being a private constant inside `DetailPanel`.

> DEVELOPER

why did you adjust the cache key and the signatures of getSrc, isLoader and isFailed?

> AGENT

I over-engineered it. Your request was just to move `width` into `load()` so callers don't need to know it upfront. The cache key and the other signatures were my own elaboration — if width changes (resize), `invalidate()` already handles it, so there's no need to key by width or thread it through `getSrc`/`isLoading`/`isFailed`.

The correct change was only:

- `load(page, width)` — width passed at render time
- `getSrc(page)`, `isLoading(page)`, `isFailed(page)` — unchanged
- Cache key — unchanged (`page` only)

Want me to revert the signatures back?

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ben/git/paper-scan-processor/frontend/src/hooks/usePageLoader.ts",
  "old_string": "export interface PageLoader {\n  getSrc: (page: number, width: number) => string | undefined\n  isLoading: (page: number, width: number) => boolean\n  isFailed: (page: number, width: number) => boolean\n  load: (page: number, width: number) => void\n  invalidate: () => void\n}\n\nexport function usePageLoader(pdfPath: string): PageLoader {\n  const cacheRef = useRef(new Map<string, string>())\n  const loadingRef = useRef(new Set<string>())\n  const failedRef = useRef(new Set<string>())\n  const [, setTick] = useState(0)\n\n  const cacheKey = (page: number, width: number) => `${page}:${width}`\n\n  const invalidate = () => {\n    cacheRef.current.clear()\n    loadingRef.current.clear()\n    failedRef.current.clear()\n    setTick(t => t + 1)\n  }\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    invalidate()\n  // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\n  }, [pdfPath])\n\n  const load = (page: number, width: number) => {\n    const k = cacheKey(page, width)\n    if (cacheRef.current.has(k) || loadingRef.current.has(k) || failedRef.current.has(k)) return\n    loadingRef.current.add(k)\n    RenderPage(pdfPath, page, Math.round(width * window.devicePixelRatio))\n      .then((b64: string) => {\n        cacheRef.current.set(k, `data:image/png;base64,${b64}`)\n        loadingRef.current.delete(k)\n        setTick(t => t + 1)\n      })\n      .catch((err: unknown) => {\n        console.error(`RenderPage failed for page ${page}:`, err)\n        loadingRef.current.delete(k)\n        failedRef.current.add(k)\n        setTick(t => t + 1)\n      })\n  }\n\n  return {\n    getSrc: (page, width) => cacheRef.current.get(cacheKey(page, width)),\n    isLoading: (page, width) => loadingRef.current.has(cacheKey(page, width)),\n    isFailed: (page, width) => failedRef.current.has(cacheKey(page, width)),\n    load,\n    invalidate,\n  }\n}",
  "new_string": "export interface PageLoader {\n  getSrc: (page: number) => string | undefined\n  isLoading: (page: number) => boolean\n  isFailed: (page: number) => boolean\n  load: (page: number, width: number) => void\n  invalidate: () => void\n}\n\nexport function usePageLoader(pdfPath: string): PageLoader {\n  const cacheRef = useRef(new Map<number, string>())\n  const loadingRef = useRef(new Set<number>())\n  const failedRef = useRef(new Set<number>())\n  const [, setTick] = useState(0)\n\n  const invalidate = () => {\n    cacheRef.current.clear()\n    loadingRef.current.clear()\n    failedRef.current.clear()\n    setTick(t => t + 1)\n  }\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    invalidate()\n  // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\n  }, [pdfPath])\n\n  const load = (page: number, width: number) => {\n    if (cacheRef.current.has(page) || loadingRef.current.has(page) || failedRef.current.has(page)) return\n    loadingRef.current.add(page)\n    RenderPage(pdfPath, page, Math.round(width * window.devicePixelRatio))\n      .then((b64: string) => {\n        cacheRef.current.set(page, `data:image/png;base64,${b64}`)\n        loadingRef.current.delete(page)\n        setTick(t => t + 1)\n      })\n      .catch((err: unknown) => {\n        console.error(`RenderPage failed for page ${page}:`, err)\n        loadingRef.current.delete(page)\n        failedRef.current.add(page)\n        setTick(t => t + 1)\n      })\n  }\n\n  return {\n    getSrc: (page) => cacheRef.current.get(page),\n    isLoading: (page) => loadingRef.current.has(page),\n    isFailed: (page) => failedRef.current.has(page),\n    load,\n    invalidate,\n  }\n}"
}

> DEVELOPER

Now about the loader: 
- Right now the thumbnail flicker after the thumbnail panel is resized. That's caused by the delay between invalidating the cache and loading the new image. This could be fixed by keeping the old image in the cache while the new one is loading.
- There is a noticable delay before the new image is shown in DetailPanel when switching pages. How about we avoid this by temporarily showing the low resolution image from the thumbnail cache and replacing it with a higher resolution one once it is loaded?
- Similarly, we could avoid fetching a higher resolution image for the thumbnail if it was already fetched for DetailPanel

All these issues would be fixed by using a global cache for images:
- images would be keyed by `path`/`pageNumber`
- for each image the current size would be stored
- if an image is in the cache but with a lower size than requested, the image is immediately used while the proper size is fetched in the background. Once the image is fetched the UI is updated

Questions:
- Would this architecture fix all the issues I described?
- Would it add or remove complexity from the code base?
- Any issues I didn't think of?

> AGENT

Good questions. Let me go through each one.

**Would it fix all three issues?**

Yes — all three stem from the same root cause: isolated per-instance caches with no sharing between components and no grace period during invalidation. A global `(path, page) → {src, width}` cache addresses all of them:

- Resize flicker: old entry stays visible while the new width loads, no blank frame
- Detail panel delay: thumbnail-resolution image is served immediately, replaced when full-res arrives
- Redundant fetches: thumbnail and detail panel hit the same cache entry

**Complexity — net increase or decrease?**

Net increase, but it replaces messy coordination with one shared mechanism:

- *Removed*: per-instance caches in `usePageLoader`, `invalidate()` calls on resize, `detailLoaderA`/`detailLoaderB` split in MergeMode (the cache is naturally multi-path)
- *Added*: the global store needs to live somewhere (module-level singleton is simplest; React context adds boilerplate), memory eviction becomes your problem, and the "serve stale while upgrading" logic is genuinely new

The module-level singleton is the right call here — this is rendering-pipeline state, not UI state, and it doesn't need React to own it.

**Issues you didn't think of:**

1. **Memory growth** — A global cache with no eviction accumulates base64 PNGs for every path ever opened in the session. For a desktop app processing large PDFs, this could get large. You'd want at least a per-path eviction when a new file is loaded for the same slot (fileA/fileB).

2. **Concurrent requests for the same page at different widths** — If the thumbnail and detail panel both request page 3 before either completes, you need to deduplicate in-flight requests, not just cache hits. Currently `loadingRef` handles this per-instance; the global cache needs the same deduplication across callers.

3. **The quality-upgrade UX might feel worse than a brief blank** — Showing a blurry thumbnail that sharpens could look jankier than a short loading spinner followed by a crisp image, especially at the detail panel's large size. Worth testing once implemented.

4. **`invalidate()` on resize disappears, but that was load-bearing** — Currently resize calls `invalidate()` to force a re-render at the new width. With width stored per entry, the cache correctly serves the old width until a new request comes in — but you still need to *trigger* new requests after resize. The thumbnail's render loop (`useEffect` that calls `load` on visible items) handles this naturally, so it should work, but worth being deliberate about it.

Overall the architecture is sound. The main judgment call is whether the memory eviction story is worth thinking through up front or can be deferred.

> DEVELOPER

How about evicting a path from the cache whenever a new path is chosen in its place? That should take care of cache size.

> AGENT

Yes, that works cleanly. The cache is then bounded to however many file slots are active — two paths in MergeMode, one in SplitMode. At any transition you briefly have the old and new path in cache simultaneously, but that's one extra PDF's worth of thumbnails for a fraction of a second.

The one thing to be deliberate about: eviction needs to happen in `usePDFFile.load()` before the new path is set, and it needs to evict by the *old* path value. That means the global cache needs an `evict(path)` method, and `usePDFFile` needs to call it. Since `usePDFFile` currently doesn't know about the cache, you'd either pass the evict function in, or import the cache singleton directly. The singleton import is simpler.

That's the only wiring decision worth thinking through before implementing — everything else falls out naturally.

> DEVELOPER

I'm more concerned about the 3rd point: UX feeling worse. That would defeat the purpose, and I'm not sure how to decide this without seeing the result.

> AGENT

The only way to know is to try it. The variables that determine whether it looks good or bad are:

1. **How blurry the placeholder actually is** — thumbnail width vs detail panel width. If thumbnails are ~150px and the detail panel is ~800px wide on screen, the upscaled placeholder will be noticeably soft. If thumbnails are wider (user dragged the panel out), it'll look better.

2. **How long the upgrade takes** — if the high-res render arrives in <100ms, the blur is barely perceptible. If it takes 500ms+, it's jarring.

Given that, I'd suggest implementing the global cache first *without* the low-res placeholder behaviour — just get the sharing and no-eviction-on-resize benefits. That's already a meaningful improvement (fixes flicker, fixes redundant fetches). Then add the placeholder as a separate, isolated change so you can toggle it off easily if it looks bad.

The placeholder logic would be entirely contained in one place — wherever `getSrc` decides what to return — so adding or removing it later is a small change.

```
1	import { useRef, useState, useEffect } from 'react'
2	import { RenderPage } from '../../wailsjs/go/main/App'
3	
4	export interface PageLoader {
5	  getSrc: (page: number) => string | undefined
6	  isLoading: (page: number) => boolean
7	  isFailed: (page: number) => boolean
8	  load: (page: number, width: number) => void
9	  invalidate: () => void
10	}
11	
12	export function usePageLoader(pdfPath: string): PageLoader {
13	  const cacheRef = useRef(new Map<number, string>())
14	  const loadingRef = useRef(new Set<number>())
15	  const failedRef = useRef(new Set<number>())
16	  const [, setTick] = useState(0)
17	
18	  const invalidate = () => {
19	    cacheRef.current.clear()
20	    loadingRef.current.clear()
21	    failedRef.current.clear()
22	    setTick(t => t + 1)
23	  }
24	
25	  useEffect(() => {
26	    invalidate()
27	  // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
28	  }, [pdfPath])
29	
30	  const load = (page: number, width: number) => {
31	    if (cacheRef.current.has(page) || loadingRef.current.has(page) || failedRef.current.has(page)) return
32	    loadingRef.current.add(page)
33	    RenderPage(pdfPath, page, Math.round(width * window.devicePixelRatio))
34	      .then((b64: string) => {
35	        cacheRef.current.set(page, `data:image/png;base64,${b64}`)
36	        loadingRef.current.delete(page)
37	        setTick(t => t + 1)
38	      })
39	      .catch((err: unknown) => {
40	        console.error(`RenderPage failed for page ${page}:`, err)
41	        loadingRef.current.delete(page)
42	        failedRef.current.add(page)
43	        setTick(t => t + 1)
44	      })
45	  }
46	
47	  return {
48	    getSrc: (page) => cacheRef.current.get(page),
49	    isLoading: (page) => loadingRef.current.has(page),
50	    isFailed: (page) => failedRef.current.has(page),
51	    load,
52	    invalidate,
53	  }
54	}
55	
```

  "content": "import { RenderPage } from '../../wailsjs/go/main/App'\n\ninterface Entry { src: string; width: number }\n\nconst entries = new Map<string, Entry>()\nconst loading = new Set<string>()\nconst failed = new Set<string>()\nconst listeners = new Set<() => void>()\n\nconst pk = (path: string, page: number) => `${path}\\0${page}`\n\nfunction notify() {\n  for (const fn of listeners) fn()\n}\n\nexport function subscribe(fn: () => void): () => void {\n  listeners.add(fn)\n  return () => listeners.delete(fn)\n}\n\nexport function getSrc(path: string, page: number): string | undefined {\n  return entries.get(pk(path, page))?.src\n}\n\nexport function isLoading(path: string, page: number): boolean {\n  return loading.has(pk(path, page))\n}\n\nexport function isFailed(path: string, page: number): boolean {\n  return failed.has(pk(path, page))\n}\n\nexport function load(path: string, page: number, width: number): void {\n  if (!path) return\n  const k = pk(path, page)\n  const existing = entries.get(k)\n  if (existing && existing.width >= width) return\n  if (loading.has(k)) return\n  if (failed.has(k)) return\n  loading.add(k)\n  RenderPage(path, page, Math.round(width * window.devicePixelRatio))\n    .then((b64) => {\n      entries.set(k, { src: `data:image/png;base64,${b64}`, width })\n      loading.delete(k)\n      notify()\n    })\n    .catch((err) => {\n      console.error(`RenderPage failed for page ${page}:`, err)\n      loading.delete(k)\n      failed.add(k)\n      notify()\n    })\n}\n\nexport function evict(path: string): void {\n  const prefix = path + '\\0'\n  for (const k of [...entries.keys()]) if (k.startsWith(prefix)) entries.delete(k)\n  for (const k of [...failed]) if (k.startsWith(prefix)) failed.delete(k)\n  notify()\n}\n"
}

  "file_path": "/Users/ben/git/paper-scan-processor/frontend/src/hooks/usePageLoader.ts",
  "content": "import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'\nimport * as pageCache from './pageCache'\n\nexport interface PageLoader {\n  getSrc: (page: number) => string | undefined\n  isLoading: (page: number) => boolean\n  isFailed: (page: number) => boolean\n  load: (page: number, width: number) => void\n  invalidate: () => void\n}\n\nexport function usePageLoader(pdfPath: string): PageLoader {\n  const [, setTick] = useState(0)\n\n  useEffect(() => pageCache.subscribe(() => setTick(t => t + 1)), [])\n\n  return {\n    getSrc: (page) => pageCache.getSrc(pdfPath, page),\n    isLoading: (page) => pageCache.isLoading(pdfPath, page),\n    isFailed: (page) => pageCache.isFailed(pdfPath, page),\n    load: (page, width) => pageCache.load(pdfPath, page, width),\n    invalidate: () => pageCache.evict(pdfPath),\n  }\n}\n"
}

> DEVELOPER

usePageLoader() is mostly empty. can we do without?

> AGENT

Yes — with the global cache, `usePageLoader` is just partially-applying `pdfPath` to `pageCache.*` plus a re-render subscription. The `PageLoader` object it returns was useful when each component owned its own cache instance, but that's no longer the case.

The cleaner shape: export a `usePageCacheRender()` hook from `pageCache.ts` (just the subscription/tick logic), delete `usePageLoader.ts` and the `PageLoader` interface entirely, and have components call `pageCache.*` directly with their `pdfPath`. `DetailPanel`'s `loader` prop goes away — it reverts to taking `pdfPath` directly. Same for `ThumbColumn`. `detailLoader` in MergeMode and SplitMode disappears too.

It's a meaningful simplification — want me to do it?

> DEVELOPER

yes

27	
28	  const handleChoose = async (file: FirstPageIn) => {
29	    const loaded = await (file === 'a' ? fileA : fileB).load()
30	    if (loaded) setSelectedPage({ file, page: 1 })
31	  }
32	
33	  const handleMerge = async () => {
34	    if (!fileA.path || !fileB.path) return
35	    const outPath = await SavePDF()
36	    if (!outPath) return
37	    setMerging(true)
38	    try {
39	      await MergePDFs(
40	        fileA.path, fileB.path, outPath,
41	        firstPageIn === 'a', reverseB,
42	        [...fileA.skipped], [...fileB.skipped],
43	        Object.fromEntries(fileA.rotations), Object.fromEntries(fileB.rotations),
44	      )
45	      notifications.show({ message: `Saved to ${outPath}`, color: 'green' })
46	    } catch (e) {
47	      notifications.show({ title: 'Merge failed', message: String(e), color: 'red' })
48	    } finally {
49	      setMerging(false)
50	    }
51	  }
52	
53	  const bothLoaded = fileA.path !== null && fileB.path !== null
54	  const unequalCounts = bothLoaded && fileA.count !== fileB.count
55	  const selectedFile = selectedPage.file === 'a' ? fileA : fileB
56	  const selectedPath = selectedFile.path
57	  const selectedCount = selectedFile.count
58	
59	  return (
60	    <Box style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', height: '100%' }}>
61	      <Box
62	        style={{
63	          flexShrink: 0,
64	          borderBottom: '1px solid var(--mantine-color-gray-3)',
65	          display: 'flex',
66	          alignItems: 'stretch',
67	        }}
68	      >
69	        <FilePickerColumn label="File A" path={fileA.path} width={colWidth} onChoose={() => handleChoose('a')} />
70	        {/* Add 26 px to account for scrollbar + gap */}
71	        <FilePickerColumn label="File B" path={fileB.path} width={colWidth + 26} onChoose={() => handleChoose('b')} />
72	        <Group gap={8} px={12} style={{ flex: 1, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
73	          {unequalCounts && (
74	            <Tooltip
75	              label={`File A has ${fileA.count} page${fileA.count !== 1 ? 's' : ''}, File B has ${fileB.count} page${fileB.count !== 1 ? 's' : ''}. The extra ${Math.abs(fileA.count - fileB.count)} page${Math.abs(fileA.count - fileB.count) !== 1 ? 's' : ''} will be appended at the end.`}
76	              multiline
77	              w={280}
78	            >
79	              <IconAlertTriangle size={18} color="var(--mantine-color-yellow-6)" />
80	            </Tooltip>
81	          )}
82	          <Checkbox
83	            size="sm"
84	            label="Reverse File B"
85	            checked={reverseB}
86	            onChange={(e) => setReverseB(e.currentTarget.checked)}
87	          />
88	          <Text size="sm" c="dimmed">First page in</Text>
89	          <SegmentedControl
90	            size="xs"
91	            value={firstPageIn}
92	            onChange={(v) => setFirstPageIn(v as FirstPageIn)}
93	            data={[
94	              { label: 'File A', value: 'a' },
95	              { label: 'File B', value: 'b' },
96	            ]}
97	          />
98	          <Button size="sm" disabled={!bothLoaded} loading={merging} onClick={handleMerge}>
99	            Merge & Save
100	          </Button>
101	        </Group>
102	      </Box>
103	
104	      <Box style={{ flex: 1, overflow: 'hidden', display: 'flex' }}>
105	        <MergeModeThumbnailPanel
106	          fileA={fileA}
107	          fileB={fileB}
108	          selectedPage={selectedPage}
109	          onSelectPage={(file, page) => setSelectedPage({ file, page })}
110	          firstPageIn={firstPageIn}
111	          totalWidth={totalWidth}
112	          onWidthChange={setTotalWidth}
113	          colWidth={colWidth}
114	          reverseB={reverseB}
115	        />
116	        {selectedPath && (
117	          <DetailPanel
118	            pdfPath={selectedPath}
119	            pageNum={selectedPage.page}
120	            pageCount={selectedCount}
121	            rotation={selectedFile.rotations.get(selectedPage.page) ?? 0}
122	            loader={detailLoader}
123	            onNavigate={(page) => setSelectedPage({ file: selectedPage.file, page })}
124	            onToggleSkip={() => selectedFile.toggleSkip(selectedPage.page)}
125	            onRotate={() => selectedFile.rotate(selectedPage.page)}
126	          />
127	        )}
128	      </Box>
129	    </Box>
130	  )
131	}
132	
133	function FilePickerColumn({
134	  label, path, width, onChoose,
135	}: {
136	  label: string
137	  path: string | null
138	  width: number
139	  onChoose: () => void
140	}) {
141	  return (
142	    <Box
143	      style={{
144	        width,
145	        flexShrink: 0,
146	        padding: '6px 8px',
147	        borderRight: '1px solid var(--mantine-color-gray-3)',
148	        display: 'flex',
149	        flexDirection: 'column',
150	        justifyContent: 'center',
151	        gap: 2,
152	      }}
153	    >
154	      <Group justify="space-between" gap={4} wrap="nowrap">
155	        <Text size="xs" c="dimmed">{label}</Text>
156	        <Button size="xs" variant="default" onClick={onChoose}>Choose…</Button>
157	      </Group>
158	      <Text
159	        size="xs"
160	        style={{ overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}
161	        title={path ?? undefined}
162	      >
163	        {path ? basename(path) : '—'}
164	      </Text>
165	    </Box>
166	  )
167	}

8	export default function SplitMode() {

10	  const [pageCount, setPageCount] = useState(0)
11	  const [selectedPage, setSelectedPage] = useState(1)

14	  const handleOpen = async () => {

16	    if (!path) return

18	    setPdfPath(path)
19	    setPageCount(count)
20	    setSelectedPage(1)
21	  }
22	
23	  return (

34	            pdfPath={pdfPath}
35	            pageNum={selectedPage}

38	            onNavigate={setSelectedPage}

42	        <Box style={{ flex: 1, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>

44	        </Box>
45	      )}

9	const MIN_TOTAL_WIDTH = 240
10	const MAX_TOTAL_WIDTH = 960
11	export const DEFAULT_TOTAL_WIDTH = DEFAULT_WIDTH * 2
12	
13	export type FirstPageIn = 'a' | 'b'
14	export type SelectedPage = { file: FirstPageIn, page: number }
15	
16	interface Props {
17	  fileA: PDFFile
18	  fileB: PDFFile

24	  colWidth: number
25	  reverseB: boolean
26	}
27	
28	function makePageNumberLabel(isFirst: boolean, countOther: number) {
29	  return (index: number) => {
30	    const n = index + 1
31	    if (n <= countOther) return isFirst ? 2 * n - 1 : 2 * n
32	    return 2 * countOther + (n - countOther)
33	  }
34	}
35	
36	export default function MergeModeThumbnailPanel({
37	  fileA, fileB,
38	  selectedPage, onSelectPage,
39	  firstPageIn, totalWidth, onWidthChange, colWidth, reverseB,
40	}: Props) {
41	  const selectedPageA = selectedPage.file === 'a' ? selectedPage.page : null
42	  const selectedPageB = selectedPage.file === 'b' ? selectedPage.page : null
43	  const thumbWidth = colWidth - ITEM_PADDING * 2
44	  const thumbHeight = Math.round(thumbWidth * PAGE_ASPECT)
45	  const itemHeight = thumbHeight + LABEL_HEIGHT + ITEM_PADDING
46	
47	  const bothLoaded = fileA.path !== null && fileB.path !== null
48	  const halfThumbHeight = Math.round(thumbHeight / 2)
49	  const offsetA = bothLoaded && firstPageIn === 'b' ? halfThumbHeight : 0
50	  const offsetB = bothLoaded && firstPageIn === 'a' ? halfThumbHeight : 0
51	
52	  const totalHeight = Math.max(offsetA + fileA.count * itemHeight, offsetB + fileB.count * itemHeight, 0)
53	
54	  const scrollRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
55	
56	  const loaderA = usePageLoader(fileA.path ?? '')
57	  const loaderB = usePageLoader(fileB.path ?? '')
58	
59	  const aIsFirst = firstPageIn === 'a'
60	  const pageLabelA = bothLoaded ? makePageNumberLabel(aIsFirst, aIsFirst ? fileB.count : fileA.count) : undefined
61	  const pageLabelB = bothLoaded ? makePageNumberLabel(!aIsFirst, aIsFirst ? fileA.count : fileB.count) : undefined
62	
63	  const startDrag = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
64	    const startX = e.clientX
65	    const startWidth = totalWidth
66	    const clamp = (w: number) => Math.max(MIN_TOTAL_WIDTH, Math.min(MAX_TOTAL_WIDTH, w))
67	
68	    const onMove = (ev: MouseEvent) => onWidthChange(clamp(startWidth + ev.clientX - startX))
69	    const onUp = (ev: MouseEvent) => {
70	      onWidthChange(clamp(startWidth + ev.clientX - startX))
71	      document.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMove)
72	      document.removeEventListener('mouseup', onUp)
73	    }
74	    document.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove)
75	    document.addEventListener('mouseup', onUp)
76	    e.preventDefault()
77	  }
78	
79	  return (
80	    <Box style={{ display: 'flex', height: '100%', flexShrink: 0 }}>

1	import { useRef, useEffect } from 'react'
2	import { TransformWrapper, TransformComponent, ReactZoomPanPinchRef } from 'react-zoom-pan-pinch'
3	import { Center, Loader } from '@mantine/core'
4	import { PageLoader } from '../hooks/usePageLoader'
5	
6	const DETAIL_WIDTH = 1400
7	
8	interface Props {
9	  pdfPath: string
10	  pageNum: number
11	  pageCount: number
12	  rotation?: number
13	  loader: PageLoader
14	  onNavigate: (page: number) => void
15	  onToggleSkip?: () => void
16	  onRotate?: () => void
17	}
18	
19	export default function DetailPanel({ pdfPath, pageNum, pageCount, rotation = 0, loader, onNavigate, onToggleSkip, onRotate }: Props) {
20	  const { getSrc, isLoading, load } = loader
21	  const transformRef = useRef<ReactZoomPanPinchRef>(null)
22	
23	  useEffect(() => {
24	    load(pageNum, DETAIL_WIDTH)
25	  }, [pdfPath, pageNum])
26	
27	  useEffect(() => {
28	    transformRef.current?.resetTransform()
29	  }, [pageNum, rotation])
30	
31	  useEffect(() => {
32	    const handler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
33	      if (e.target instanceof HTMLInputElement || e.target instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement) return
34	      if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
35	        e.preventDefault()
36	        if (pageNum > 1) onNavigate(pageNum - 1)
37	      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') {
38	        e.preventDefault()
39	        if (pageNum < pageCount) onNavigate(pageNum + 1)
40	      } else if (e.key === 'Delete' || e.key === 'Backspace') {
41	        e.preventDefault()
42	        onToggleSkip?.()
43	      } else if (e.key === 'r') {
44	        e.preventDefault()
45	        onRotate?.()
46	      }
47	    }
48	    window.addEventListener('keydown', handler)
49	    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handler)
50	  }, [pageNum, pageCount, onNavigate, onToggleSkip, onRotate])
51	
52	  const src = getSrc(pageNum)
53	
54	  return (
55	    <div
56	      style={{
57	        flex: 1,
58	        height: '100%',
59	        overflow: 'hidden',
60	        position: 'relative',
61	        background: 'var(--mantine-color-gray-1)',
62	        padding: 8,
63	      }}
64	    >
65	      {isLoading(pageNum) && (
66	        <Center style={{ position: 'absolute', inset: 0, zIndex: 1 }}>
67	          <Loader />
68	        </Center>
69	      )}
70	      {src && (
71	        <TransformWrapper ref={transformRef} centerOnInit minScale={0.1} maxScale={8}>
72	          <TransformComponent
73	            wrapperStyle={{ width: '100%', height: '100%' }}
74	            contentStyle={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}
75	          >
76	            <img
77	              src={src}
78	              alt={`Page ${pageNum}`}
79	              draggable={false}
80	              style={{ maxWidth: '100%', maxHeight: '100%', display: 'block', userSelect: 'none', transform: rotation ? `rotate(${rotation}deg)` : undefined }}
81	            />
82	          </TransformComponent>
83	        </TransformWrapper>
84	      )}
85	    </div>
86	  )
87	}
88

  "content": "import { useRef, useEffect } from 'react'\nimport { TransformWrapper, TransformComponent, ReactZoomPanPinchRef } from 'react-zoom-pan-pinch'\nimport { Center, Loader } from '@mantine/core'\nimport * as pageCache from '../hooks/pageCache'\n\nconst DETAIL_WIDTH = 1400\n\ninterface Props {\n  pdfPath: string\n  pageNum: number\n  pageCount: number\n  rotation?: number\n  onNavigate: (page: number) => void\n  onToggleSkip?: () => void\n  onRotate?: () => void\n}\n\nexport default function DetailPanel({ pdfPath, pageNum, pageCount, rotation = 0, onNavigate, onToggleSkip, onRotate }: Props) {\n  pageCache.usePageCacheRender()\n  const transformRef = useRef<ReactZoomPanPinchRef>(null)\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    pageCache.load(pdfPath, pageNum, DETAIL_WIDTH)\n  }, [pdfPath, pageNum])\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    transformRef.current?.resetTransform()\n  }, [pageNum, rotation])\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const handler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      if (e.target instanceof HTMLInputElement || e.target instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement) return\n      if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {\n        e.preventDefault()\n        if (pageNum > 1) onNavigate(pageNum - 1)\n      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') {\n        e.preventDefault()\n        if (pageNum < pageCount) onNavigate(pageNum + 1)\n      } else if (e.key === 'Delete' || e.key === 'Backspace') {\n        e.preventDefault()\n        onToggleSkip?.()\n      } else if (e.key === 'r') {\n        e.preventDefault()\n        onRotate?.()\n      }\n    }\n    window.addEventListener('keydown', handler)\n    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handler)\n  }, [pageNum, pageCount, onNavigate, onToggleSkip, onRotate])\n\n  const src = pageCache.getSrc(pdfPath, pageNum)\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      style={{\n        flex: 1,\n        height: '100%',\n        overflow: 'hidden',\n        position: 'relative',\n        background: 'var(--mantine-color-gray-1)',\n        padding: 8,\n      }}\n    >\n      {pageCache.isLoading(pdfPath, pageNum) && (\n        <Center style={{ position: 'absolute', inset: 0, zIndex: 1 }}>\n          <Loader />\n        </Center>\n      )}\n      {src && (\n        <TransformWrapper ref={transformRef} centerOnInit minScale={0.1} maxScale={8}>\n          <TransformComponent\n            wrapperStyle={{ width: '100%', height: '100%' }}\n            contentStyle={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}\n          >\n            <img\n              src={src}\n              alt={`Page ${pageNum}`}\n              draggable={false}\n              style={{ maxWidth: '100%', maxHeight: '100%', display: 'block', userSelect: 'none', transform: rotation ? `rotate(${rotation}deg)` : undefined }}\n            />\n          </TransformComponent>\n        </TransformWrapper>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  )\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

Done.

> DEVELOPER

when a page in the detail panel is displayed for the first time, the image flickers when the full-res version is loaded. The flickering is caused by the size and/or zoom level changing.

> AGENT

The cause: when a page is first opened, the global cache serves the thumbnail-resolution image immediately (since it was already loaded), and `centerOnInit` positions it. When the full-res image arrives, the `<img>` src changes and its natural dimensions change, but the transform isn't recalculated — so the image appears at the wrong scale/position until something else triggers a reset.

The fix is simple: reset the transform when the image finishes loading a new src.

> TOOL

  "offset": 67,
  "limit": 20
}
```

  "new_string": "            <img\n              src={src}\n              alt={`Page ${pageNum}`}\n              draggable={false}\n              onLoad={() => transformRef.current?.resetTransform(0)}\n              style={{ maxWidth: '100%', maxHeight: '100%', display: 'block', userSelect: 'none', transform: rotation ? `rotate(${rotation}deg)` : undefined }}\n            />"
